# Graph Report - linorobot2  (2026-07-08)

## Corpus Check
- 2495 files · ~3,006,664 words
- Verdict: corpus is large enough that graph structure adds value.

## Summary
- 14110 nodes · 35792 edges · 991 communities (581 shown, 410 thin omitted)
- Extraction: 85% EXTRACTED · 15% INFERRED · 0% AMBIGUOUS · INFERRED: 5229 edges (avg confidence: 0.8)
- Token cost: 0 input · 0 output

## Graph Freshness
- Built from commit: `87ea77ce`
- Run `git rev-parse HEAD` and compare to check if the graph is stale.
- Run `graphify update .` after code changes (no API cost).
